Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The detection variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 305 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 821 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to detection structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 305/821 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.
